----- MODULE CounterTieInGraph ----
-EXTENDS Naturals
-VARIABLE x
-Init == x = 0
-Inc == x < 3 /\ x' = x + 1
-Alpha == x = 0 /\ x' = 3
-Down == x > 0 /\ x' = x - 1
-Next == Inc \/ Alpha \/ Down
-Spec == Init /\ [][Next]_x
-NoDownFromTwo == ~(x = 2 /\ x' = 1)
-OneOrThree == x # 1 /\ x # 3
-====

----- MODULE CounterView ----
-EXTENDS Naturals
-VARIABLE x
-Init == x = 0
-Inc == x' = x + 1
-Jump == x = 0 /\ x' = 3
-Next == Inc \/ Jump
-Spec == Init /\ [][Next]_x
-Small == x < 5
-NoJump == ~(x = 0 /\ x' = 3)
-NotThree == x # 3
-NotFive == x # 5
-NotOdd == x % 2 = 0
-====

----- MODULE DieHard ----
-\* The classic jug puzzle from Die Hard 3: a 3-gallon jug, a 5-gallon jug,
-\* and a fountain. Get exactly 4 gallons in the big jug.
-EXTENDS Naturals
-
-VARIABLES big, small
-vars == <<big, small>>
-
-Min(m, n) == IF m < n THEN m ELSE n
-
-TypeOK == small \in 0..3 /\ big \in 0..5
-
-Init == big = 0 /\ small = 0
-
-FillSmall == small' = 3 /\ big' = big
-FillBig == big' = 5 /\ small' = small
-EmptySmall == small' = 0 /\ big' = big
-EmptyBig == big' = 0 /\ small' = small
-
-SmallToBig ==
-  /\ big' = Min(big + small, 5)
-  /\ small' = small - (big' - big)
-
-BigToSmall ==
-  /\ small' = Min(big + small, 3)
-  /\ big' = big - (small' - small)
-
-Next ==
-  \/ FillSmall \/ FillBig
-  \/ EmptySmall \/ EmptyBig
-  \/ SmallToBig \/ BigToSmall
-
-Spec == Init /\ [][Next]_vars
-
-\* The puzzle's goal, written as a rule that should break: TLC's
-\* counterexample is the solution.
-NotSolved == big # 4
-====

----- MODULE HiddenClock ----
-EXTENDS Naturals
-VARIABLES x, clock
-vars == <<x, clock>>
-Init == x = 0 /\ clock = 0
-Tick == clock < 3 /\ clock' = clock + 1 /\ x' = IF clock = 2 THEN 0 ELSE 1
-Next == Tick
-Spec == Init /\ [][Next]_vars /\ WF_vars(Next)
-OnlyX == x
-EndsAtZero == <>[](x = 0)
-====

-# HiddenClock
-
-TLC reached 2 states and 2 steps. The chart draws 2 states and 1 arrows.
-The chart does not draw 1 steps that stay in one chart state.
-With VIEW, TLC keeps one representative for each group of states that it counts as the same. An arrow can lead to the representative rather than to the state that the step reaches.
-
-## EndsAtZero breaks
-TLC's counterexample is in tlc-EndsAtZero.out. It has 1 step:
-0. x=0 clock=0
-1. Tick: x=1 clock=1
-The run stops in "x=1 clock=1" forever.

----- MODULE LoginParallel ----
-\* Generated by chart-to-tla.mjs from login-parallel.mmd.
-\* Each region is one variable and each transition label is one action.
-\* Once you add guards and variables from the real code, this file is the source.
-\* statechart.mjs from-chart login-parallel.mmd keeps it, checks it, and compares it with the chart.
-
-VARIABLES session, wrongTries
-vars == <<session, wrongTries>>
-
-\* Region Session
-sessionStates == {"Checking the password", "Signed in", "Signed out"}
-\* Region Wrong tries
-wrongTriesStates == {"1 wrong try", "2 wrong tries", "Locked out", "No wrong tries"}
-
-TypeOK ==
-    /\ session \in sessionStates
-    /\ wrongTries \in wrongTriesStates
-
-Init ==
-    /\ session = "Signed out"
-    /\ wrongTries = "No wrong tries"
-
-\* you submit a password
-YouSubmitAPassword ==
-    /\ \/ (session = "Signed out" /\ session' = "Checking the password")
-    /\ UNCHANGED <<wrongTries>>
-
-\* the password is right
-ThePasswordIsRight ==
-    /\ (session \in {"Checking the password"} \/ wrongTries \in {"1 wrong try", "2 wrong tries"})
-    /\ \/ (session = "Checking the password" /\ session' = "Signed in")
-       \/ (session \notin {"Checking the password"} /\ UNCHANGED session)
-    /\ \/ (wrongTries = "1 wrong try" /\ wrongTries' = "No wrong tries")
-       \/ (wrongTries = "2 wrong tries" /\ wrongTries' = "No wrong tries")
-       \/ (wrongTries \notin {"1 wrong try", "2 wrong tries"} /\ UNCHANGED wrongTries)
-
-\* the password is wrong
-ThePasswordIsWrong ==
-    /\ (session \in {"Checking the password"} \/ wrongTries \in {"No wrong tries", "1 wrong try", "2 wrong tries"})
-    /\ \/ (session = "Checking the password" /\ session' = "Signed out")
-       \/ (session \notin {"Checking the password"} /\ UNCHANGED session)
-    /\ \/ (wrongTries = "No wrong tries" /\ wrongTries' = "1 wrong try")
-       \/ (wrongTries = "1 wrong try" /\ wrongTries' = "2 wrong tries")
-       \/ (wrongTries = "2 wrong tries" /\ wrongTries' = "Locked out")
-       \/ (wrongTries \notin {"No wrong tries", "1 wrong try", "2 wrong tries"} /\ UNCHANGED wrongTries)
-
-\* you sign out
-YouSignOut ==
-    /\ \/ (session = "Signed in" /\ session' = "Signed out")
-    /\ UNCHANGED <<wrongTries>>
-
-\* 15 minutes pass
-S15MinutesPass ==
-    /\ \/ (wrongTries = "Locked out" /\ wrongTries' = "No wrong tries")
-    /\ UNCHANGED <<session>>
-
-Next == YouSubmitAPassword \/ ThePasswordIsRight \/ ThePasswordIsWrong \/ YouSignOut \/ S15MinutesPass
-
-Spec == Init /\ [][Next]_vars
-
-\* A locked-out account never signs in until the lockout ends.
-SignedInWhileLocked == ~(session = "Signed in" /\ wrongTries = "Locked out")
-
-====

-# MediaPlayer
-
-TLC reached 12 states and 64 steps. The chart draws 7 states and 13 arrows.
-The step a call comes in changes Playback and Volume at the same time.
-The step the track ends changes Playback and Volume at the same time.
-The step you press stop changes Playback and Volume at the same time.
-
-## LoudWhenStopped breaks
-The shortest path has 1 step:
-0. Playback Stopped, Volume Normal volume
-1. you turn it up: Playback Stopped, Volume Loud
-
-## Rules that hold
-TypeOK, NoDeadlock, EventuallyStops

----- MODULE MediaPlayerFixed ----
-\* Generated by chart-to-tla.mjs from media-player-fixed.mmd.
-\* Each region is one variable and each transition label is one action.
-\* Once you add guards and variables from the real code, this file is the source.
-\* statechart.mjs from-chart media-player-fixed.mmd keeps it, checks it, and compares it with the chart.
-
-VARIABLES playback, volume
-vars == <<playback, volume>>
-
-\* Region Playback
-playbackStates == {"Buffering", "Paused", "Playing", "Stopped"}
-\* Region Volume
-volumeStates == {"Loud", "Muted", "Normal volume"}
-
-TypeOK ==
-    /\ playback \in playbackStates
-    /\ volume \in volumeStates
-
-Init ==
-    /\ playback = "Stopped"
-    /\ volume = "Normal volume"
-
-\* you press play
-YouPressPlay ==
-    /\ \/ (playback = "Stopped" /\ playback' = "Buffering")
-       \/ (playback = "Paused" /\ playback' = "Playing")
-    /\ UNCHANGED <<volume>>
-
-\* enough data arrives
-EnoughDataArrives ==
-    /\ \/ (playback = "Buffering" /\ playback' = "Playing")
-    /\ UNCHANGED <<volume>>
-
-\* the network slows
-TheNetworkSlows ==
-    /\ \/ (playback = "Playing" /\ playback' = "Buffering")
-    /\ UNCHANGED <<volume>>
-
-\* you press pause
-YouPressPause ==
-    /\ \/ (playback = "Playing" /\ playback' = "Paused")
-    /\ UNCHANGED <<volume>>
-
-\* a call comes in
-ACallComesIn ==
-    /\ (playback \in {"Playing"} \/ volume \in {"Normal volume", "Loud"})
-    /\ \/ (playback = "Playing" /\ playback' = "Paused")
-       \/ (playback \notin {"Playing"} /\ UNCHANGED playback)
-    /\ \/ (volume = "Normal volume" /\ volume' = "Muted")
-       \/ (volume = "Loud" /\ volume' = "Muted")
-       \/ (volume \notin {"Normal volume", "Loud"} /\ UNCHANGED volume)
-
-\* the track ends
-TheTrackEnds ==
-    /\ (playback \in {"Playing"} \/ volume \in {"Loud"})
-    /\ \/ (playback = "Playing" /\ playback' = "Stopped")
-       \/ (playback \notin {"Playing"} /\ UNCHANGED playback)
-    /\ \/ (volume = "Loud" /\ volume' = "Normal volume")
-       \/ (volume \notin {"Loud"} /\ UNCHANGED volume)
-
-\* you press stop
-YouPressStop ==
-    /\ (playback \in {"Playing", "Paused", "Buffering"} \/ volume \in {"Loud"})
-    /\ \/ (playback = "Playing" /\ playback' = "Stopped")
-       \/ (playback = "Paused" /\ playback' = "Stopped")
-       \/ (playback = "Buffering" /\ playback' = "Stopped")
-       \/ (playback \notin {"Playing", "Paused", "Buffering"} /\ UNCHANGED playback)
-    /\ \/ (volume = "Loud" /\ volume' = "Normal volume")
-       \/ (volume \notin {"Loud"} /\ UNCHANGED volume)
-
-\* you turn it up
-YouTurnItUp ==
-    /\ \/ (volume = "Normal volume" /\ ~(playback = "Stopped") /\ volume' = "Loud")
-    /\ UNCHANGED <<playback>>
-
-\* you turn it down
-YouTurnItDown ==
-    /\ \/ (volume = "Loud" /\ volume' = "Normal volume")
-    /\ UNCHANGED <<playback>>
-
-\* you press mute
-YouPressMute ==
-    /\ \/ (volume = "Normal volume" /\ volume' = "Muted")
-       \/ (volume = "Loud" /\ volume' = "Muted")
-       \/ (volume = "Muted" /\ volume' = "Normal volume")
-    /\ UNCHANGED <<playback>>
-
-\* the call ends
-TheCallEnds ==
-    /\ \/ (volume = "Muted" /\ volume' = "Normal volume")
-    /\ UNCHANGED <<playback>>
-
-Next == YouPressPlay \/ EnoughDataArrives \/ TheNetworkSlows \/ YouPressPause \/ ACallComesIn \/ TheTrackEnds \/ YouPressStop \/ YouTurnItUp \/ YouTurnItDown \/ YouPressMute \/ TheCallEnds
-
-Spec == Init /\ [][Next]_vars
-
-\* When nothing plays, the volume is never above the safe level.
-LoudWhenStopped == ~(playback = "Stopped" /\ volume = "Loud")
-

----- MODULE MediaPlayer ----
-\* Generated by chart-to-tla.mjs from media-player-fixed.mmd.
-\* Each region is one variable and each transition label is one action.
-\* Once you add guards and variables from the real code, this file is the source.
-\* statechart.mjs from-chart media-player-fixed.mmd keeps it, checks it, and compares it with the chart.
-
-VARIABLES playback, volume
-vars == <<playback, volume>>
-
-\* Region Playback
-playbackStates == {"Buffering", "Paused", "Playing", "Stopped"}
-\* Region Volume
-volumeStates == {"Loud", "Muted", "Normal volume"}
-
-TypeOK ==
-    /\ playback \in playbackStates
-    /\ volume \in volumeStates
-
-Init ==
-    /\ playback = "Stopped"
-    /\ volume = "Normal volume"
-
-\* you press play
-YouPressPlay ==
-    /\ \/ (playback = "Stopped" /\ playback' = "Buffering")
-       \/ (playback = "Paused" /\ playback' = "Playing")
-    /\ UNCHANGED <<volume>>
-
-\* enough data arrives
-EnoughDataArrives ==
-    /\ \/ (playback = "Buffering" /\ playback' = "Playing")
-    /\ UNCHANGED <<volume>>
-
-\* the network slows
-TheNetworkSlows ==
-    /\ \/ (playback = "Playing" /\ playback' = "Buffering")
-    /\ UNCHANGED <<volume>>
-
-\* you press pause
-YouPressPause ==
-    /\ \/ (playback = "Playing" /\ playback' = "Paused")
-    /\ UNCHANGED <<volume>>
-
-\* a call comes in
-ACallComesIn ==
-    /\ (playback \in {"Playing"} \/ volume \in {"Normal volume", "Loud"})
-    /\ \/ (playback = "Playing" /\ playback' = "Paused")
-       \/ (playback \notin {"Playing"} /\ UNCHANGED playback)
-    /\ \/ (volume = "Normal volume" /\ volume' = "Muted")
-       \/ (volume = "Loud" /\ volume' = "Muted")
-       \/ (volume \notin {"Normal volume", "Loud"} /\ UNCHANGED volume)
-
-\* the track ends
-TheTrackEnds ==
-    /\ (playback \in {"Playing"} \/ volume \in {"Loud"})
-    /\ \/ (playback = "Playing" /\ playback' = "Stopped")
-       \/ (playback \notin {"Playing"} /\ UNCHANGED playback)
-    /\ \/ (volume = "Loud" /\ volume' = "Normal volume")
-       \/ (volume \notin {"Loud"} /\ UNCHANGED volume)
-
-\* you press stop
-YouPressStop ==
-    /\ (playback \in {"Playing", "Paused", "Buffering"} \/ volume \in {"Loud"})
-    /\ \/ (playback = "Playing" /\ playback' = "Stopped")
-       \/ (playback = "Paused" /\ playback' = "Stopped")
-       \/ (playback = "Buffering" /\ playback' = "Stopped")
-       \/ (playback \notin {"Playing", "Paused", "Buffering"} /\ UNCHANGED playback)
-    /\ \/ (volume = "Loud" /\ volume' = "Normal volume")
-       \/ (volume \notin {"Loud"} /\ UNCHANGED volume)
-
-\* you turn it up
-YouTurnItUp ==
-    /\ \/ (volume = "Normal volume" /\ volume' = "Loud")
-    /\ UNCHANGED <<playback>>
-
-\* you turn it down
-YouTurnItDown ==
-    /\ \/ (volume = "Loud" /\ volume' = "Normal volume")
-    /\ UNCHANGED <<playback>>
-
-\* you press mute
-YouPressMute ==
-    /\ \/ (volume = "Normal volume" /\ volume' = "Muted")
-       \/ (volume = "Loud" /\ volume' = "Muted")
-       \/ (volume = "Muted" /\ volume' = "Normal volume")
-    /\ UNCHANGED <<playback>>
-
-\* the call ends
-TheCallEnds ==
-    /\ \/ (volume = "Muted" /\ volume' = "Normal volume")
-    /\ UNCHANGED <<playback>>
-
-Next == YouPressPlay \/ EnoughDataArrives \/ TheNetworkSlows \/ YouPressPause \/ ACallComesIn \/ TheTrackEnds \/ YouPressStop \/ YouTurnItUp \/ YouTurnItDown \/ YouPressMute \/ TheCallEnds
-
-Spec == Init /\ [][Next]_vars
-
-\* The player stops at last.
-EventuallyStops == <>(playback = "Stopped")
-
-\* When nothing plays, the volume is never above the safe level.
-LoudWhenStopped == ~(playback = "Stopped" /\ volume = "Loud")
-
-====
